PETRI v6.1 SIMULATION: WHAT THE v6.1 RULES DO, AND WHAT CHANGED FROM v6 (sim/out-v6.1, run from sim/:
`cargo run --release -- out-v6.1 all --seeds 8`, 91 s on 12 threads; re-run on v6.1 part 2: the kept rule and pacts)

Both rule files were loaded exactly (contract/launch-rules.json for testnet, contract/launch-rules-mainnet.json for mainnet, both now with
"licence_min_cells": 12). Their economic parameters are identical (checked field by field); they differ only in the house, keeper and wNEAR
account names. Every v6.1 run mirrors contract v6.1: the hot table holds only colonies that earned since the last tide or have living cells and
a stake that can still earn (>= 125 at reach 8; never the house), at most 10,000 rows, 32 leaving a call; stake waits in a 69-byte cold slot
(pages of 32) and decays lazily; the catch-up reserves the purse part of the table only when a tide falls in it (limit min(110, prepaid - 40)
less what is still to write); PETRI amounts in entries are u64 and a tide stores 130 bytes (was 289); PLANT_STORAGE_BASE 180 (was 240) and
BUY_BYTES 113 (was 140); licences only for slides of >= 12 cells; royalties from planters that never attached NEAR go to the agar; the keeper
pot to any outside caller whose call ran a generation; the site prepays 300 TGas. Runs marked V6 set all of v6's rules and layout back on the
same seeds (they reproduce sim/out-v6 exactly). "Cash" = NEAR a player actually got back minus NEAR it actually paid (storage and gas
included); median and (min..max) over seeds.

WHAT v6.1 PART 2 CHANGED, MEASURED (fixes.txt P1, P2, N1, F2; section 6 (f7) and (f8) below)

  1. Free play never reaches NEAR (the kept rule). Guests that ft_transfer their farmed PETRI to a wallet that sells it: the vault paid
     +0.047 NEAR a week per operator without the rule, 0 with it (every transfer refused). Guests that attach 0.01 NEAR to sell what they
     farmed: 0 with the rule (their sales are refused until plantings spend the farm), +0.0008 without, since a day's farm is worth less
     than a sale's record. Across every colony of the attack run, vault NEAR for PETRI earned before a first NEAR: 0.167 -> 0. Everyone
     else's cash is within seed noise.
  2. Pacts the contract enforces: raiders bound by them aimed 2.0 raids a week elsewhere and had 1.6 plantings refused (150 p); a builder
     paying 2 PETRI every window for one loses 0.035 NEAR a week at that naive price, so pacts pay only against a real threat.
  3. The pulsar farmer (+0.11) is the licence working as designed: planters choose it and still save, and the vault's totals hardly move
     with the royalty (fixes.txt N1). tide_cap_bps 2500 was measured and not adopted (fixes.txt F2).

WHAT v6.1 CHANGED, MEASURED (v6 -> v6.1, same seeds; fixes.txt has the details)

  1. Gas at 30,000 colonies (2 days, 2 seeds, 427,131 plantings): hot rows avg 17,102 -> 1,336, max 27,538 -> 2,390; the largest call
     237 -> 61 TGas (a join that caught up through a tide and pays its bounty; plant 237 -> 60, buy 234 -> 55, claim 233 -> 52, sell 147 ->
     54, advance 206 -> 23; 30 TGas payout reservations included); calls over 150 TGas 116,731 -> 0; calls over their prepaid gas 16,102 -> 0;
     plantings refused because the table was full 0 (the 10,000 cap was never reached). Every catch-up can step all 120 generations at up
     to 10,000 rows; the analytic worst case (10,000 rows, every one a tide winner) is 120 TGas for the tide call, 105 for a claim with NEAR,
     77 for a plant.
  2. Tide entries: 289 -> 130 B. Tide-entry storage as a share of tide pay on the young dish: 39% -> 21% (one outsider), 31% -> 16% (5),
     8% -> 4% (20). Paid to colonies: 0.69 -> 0.80 NEAR (Penny alone), 1.00 -> 1.10 (5); with 20 outsiders 5.81 -> 5.47, because the tides
     themselves are smaller (6.32 -> 5.74 NEAR): a planting now attaches 60 fewer bytes of storage deposit (PLANT_STORAGE_BASE 240 -> 180), whose surplus fed the vault.
  3. Royalty farming: with licence_min_cells 12 the farmer can license only the pulsar of its eight patterns; licensed plantings 7,852 ->
     466 (all pulsars), royalties 6,296 -> 3,171 PETRI, farmer cash +0.29 -> +0.11 (+0.08..+0.15) NEAR a week (150 p); young 20: +0.21 ->
     +0.03 (-0.03..+0.21). Cut by about two thirds; what is left is by design (N1 in fixes.txt; licence_min_cells 49 or
     slide_royalty_bps 0 would make it lose 0.16).
  4. Guest royalty leak: royalties paid by colonies that never attached NEAR 56 -> 0 PETRI (also with the 6-cell licence kept,
     licence_min_cells 0).
  5. Keeper pot: left at the end 1,480 -> 1.1 PETRI (young 20, 7 d) and 12.3 -> 1.3 (150 p); the pot now spreads over everyone whose call
     ran a generation, never a guest (150 p medians per player: Methuselah 82, Whale 49, Locker 28, Late 21, Racer 13 PETRI); the racer's
     cash -0.019 -> -0.006.
  Economics otherwise within seed noise (150 p 7 d: Builder -0.193 -> -0.187, Whale -2.00 -> -2.01, Penny -0.043 -> -0.046, Methuselah
  +0.41 -> +0.35). The mainnet house lasts a day longer (out of NEAR on day 7 (6..7), was 6): its plantings carry less storage deposit.

THE CASH TABLE, IN PLAIN WORDS (young mainnet dish: the 5 house strains plus a few outsiders; section 1 has every number)

  Outsider type          alone, 7 d        alone, 14 d       with 5 outsiders, 7 d   with 20 outsiders, 7 d
  Penny (0.01 NEAR join, one block kept alive)
                         +0.57 NEAR [v6 +0.44]  +0.51 [+0.37]  -0.05 (all of its 0.05)  -0.05
  Methuselah (R-pentomino an hour, 0.5 budget)
                         +0.36 [+0.22]     +0.21 [+0.06]     +0.06 (-0.09..+0.33)     +0.21 (+0.10..+0.39)
  Builder (0.25 budget)  -0.25             -0.25             -0.25                    -0.25
  Raider / Defender      -                 -                 -0.25 / -0.25            -0.23 / -0.25
  Sniper                 -0.25             -0.25             -                        -0.11
  Whale (3 NEAR budget)  -3.00             -3.00             -                        -3.00
  Locker (2 NEAR)        -1.99             -2.00
  SelfSponsor (10 subs)  -0.72             -0.72
  Racer                  -0.01             -0.01

  1. The first outsider still collects every tide while it is alone (100% of ~165 tides), and now more of them: a lone Penny nets +0.57 NEAR
     (+0.54..+0.62) of the house's 2.28 NEAR in a week (v6 +0.44), because the tides store less. With 5 outsiders its share of all tide
     shares is 15% (4%..43%), with 20 under 1%. (Judgment item F2 in fixes.txt: a 25% cap per colony per tide brings it to +0.41.)
  2. Storage takes much less of a young dish's tides (above). Of all NEAR that comes in, storage is 61% with one outsider (v6 70%).
  3. The mainnet house runs dry on day 7 (6..7) at 0.55 NEAR a strain (v6: day 6); v6.1 does not change the house's pace (F8 of v6 stands).
  4. Most kinds still lose; the methuselah is still the kind ahead at 15 and 150 players (+0.38, +0.37), -0.05 at 1,500 and -0.39 over 30
     days.
  5. Free play: a guest's own account never receives NEAR, no free guest's royalty reaches a wallet (fix 4), and no guest's PETRI reaches
     a seller by transfer or by attaching a little NEAR (part 2). Royalty sybils lose
     0.10 NEAR an operator (v6 0.16): with no 6-cell licence the operator's guests plant without paying it.
  6. Gas no longer grows with the colonies that ever played: the hot table follows the colonies that earn now, ~300 alive and ~1,300 rows on
     average on an active 30,000-colony dish. The largest calls at 150 players: claim with its payout 68 TGas (v6 68), plant 39 (40), sell 34
     (67). Every call is far below 150 TGas and the 300 the site now prepays.
  7. House-transfer laundering is still impossible (0 PETRI moved to non-house accounts in 423 runs), and the keeper pot is the one path
     that changed: it goes to any registered non-house caller whose call ran a generation, never to the house or a keeper.
  8. Board saturation and the 90-day runs are as in v6 within noise (sections 3 and 4): ground is never closed, only dearer (a newcomer's
     block 3.8-5.2 mNEAR at 5,000-15,000 players, 5.2-9.3 inside a buffer); with 10 new outsiders a day the agar falls to 680,000 PETRI by
     day 90.

Rules that still look broken, and each v6.1 fix's measured effect: fixes.txt.

Petri v6.1 economy simulation (sim/src: main.rs, world.rs, model.rs) on the launch rules of both dishes (contract v6.1; v6 runs marked V6).
All amounts NEAR unless marked. 'Cash' = NEAR actually received (tide claims, sales, bounties, sponsorship taken back) minus NEAR actually paid
(attached deposits with their storage, NEAR spent sponsoring, gas at 0.0001 NEAR/TGas). Each cell: median over seeds of the kind's mean player, (min..max).
Assumptions and the storage/gas model are in section 7. The house is modelled as agents/ plays it on mainnet (section 7).

  (f6) House-transfer laundering (house PETRI or NEAR reaching a player other than through the agar and the vault) — every path, as the v6.1 code has it (unchanged from v6 except the keeper pot):
    ft_transfer / ft_transfer_call house -> player      refused: token.rs:231 require!(!is_house(from) || is_house(to)); keepers are house (lib.rs is_house)
    house sells PETRI back for vault NEAR              refused: lib.rs sell() require!(!colony.house)
    house plants a player's slide (royalty to player)  refused: lib.rs plant() require!(!colony.house) when the holder is not the planter
    house or keeper takes the keeper pot               refused: lib.rs catch_up_by pays it only to a registered caller that !is_house
    tide bounty to a house runner (colony or keeper)   none: catch_up_by house_runner -> bounty 0, stays in the vault
    tide share / feed to house colonies                none: feed_with and tide_split_sparse skip `house` ids
    council pact house -> house, then house -> player  the second hop is the ft_transfer refusal above
    house slide NFT given to a player                  allowed (nft_transfer is not restricted), but the house never plants it again and its 16x16
                                                       fossil patterns are not what anyone plants: no royalty flow. The agents never transfer slides.
    house NEAR -> vault -> tides                       by design: the house's NEAR is the players' subsidy (section 1 measures who gets it)
    In every simulated run (the sim mirrors these checks; a house licence plant asserts): house PETRI moved to non-house accounts = 0 PETRI (max over 467 runs).
  (f7) Part 2, free play reaching NEAR: 150 players + bench + 3 SybilAttach and 3 SybilTransfer operators (each 1 wallet + 10 relayer guests that
  farm blocks on free credit; testnet rules, 7 days, 6 seeds). SybilAttach guests hoard a day, attach 0.01 NEAR each (a purchase the operator pays
  for) and then sell everything after every tide while still planting; SybilTransfer guests ft_transfer all their PETRI to the wallet, which sells.
  'kept' = v6.1 part 2; 'no rule' = the same seeds without the kept rule (v6.1 part 1). Per operator (median (min..max)):
  'farmed sold' = NEAR the vault paid for PETRI earned before the seller's first NEAR, bought with credit, or moved to it by a guest's transfer.

    Under the kept rule no sale for NEAR happens while a colony holds kept PETRI and no guest transfer happens at all, so NEAR paid for PETRI earned
    before a colony's first NEAR is 0 by construction; a SybilAttach guest's sales are of PETRI it earned after attaching, once its plantings had spent
    what it farmed. Vault left at the end: kept 0.1308 (0.0751..0.1536), no rule 0.1085 (0.0686..0.2672); NEAR paid out for sales in all: kept 8.8698 (8.1971..9.2078), no rule 8.6666 (8.2585..9.1052).

  (f8) Pacts: after every tide each Builder pays the Raider nearest its home, if that raider lives within 40 cells, 2 PETRI for a pact (the raider
  may not plant within pact_radius 8 of the builder's living cells for a tide window; the builder pays the offer's storage, 0.0038 NEAR, and both calls'
  gas); the raider accepts, aims elsewhere when its pick is a protected colony, and the contract refuses the rest. 'cell-gens' = the builder's living

=== 7. Rules, storage and gas model, house model ===
Rules: ../contract/launch-rules.json (testnet) and ../contract/launch-rules-mainnet.json (mainnet), read with serde (unknown fields refused).
  Economic parameters identical in both files: YES (they differ only in house, keepers and wnear).
  128x128 dish, 15000 ms generations (5760 a day); feed_ppm 3, keeper_bps 200, reach 8, stake_keep_bps 8750, burn_bps 5000, payback 480, min_cell_price 0.5 PETRI, live_floor 1000,
  petri_price 0.0002 NEAR, max_supply 1000000 PETRI, min_join 0.01 NEAR, cell_fee 0.0001 NEAR, tide_every 240, tide_bps 500 (5% of the vault), tide_bounty_bps 100 (max 0.001 NEAR),
  sell_bps 5000, sell_cap_bps 1000, siege_x 3, buffer 3, max_plant_cells 64, max_catchup 120, max_lag 480, slide_cells_price 1 PETRI, slide_min_cells 5,
  slide_discount_bps 2500, slide_royalty_bps 1000, licence_min_cells 12, house 5 strains, keepers ["keeper.petri-dish.near"].
Storage, from the v6.1 borsh layout (key + value + 40 B per record; 1e19 yocto a byte; v6's in brackets where it differs):
  join 686 B [701] for a 16-char wallet (Colony 251 + colony_of 63 + name 57 + balance 77 + Join entry 56 + cold slot 69 [57] + 113 [140] charged for the
    Buy entry, which is 97 B, 113 with `spent` [105/121]; a new cold page of 32 [64] adds 43); buy 113 B [140] charged;
  plant_deposit = (180 [240] + 77 [first hot row] + 82 [empty table] + 4c + note+64 + licence 26+L [33+L]) B + 0.0001/cell;
  stored by a planting: Plant entry 103+2c [111+2c] (+16 spent, +42 [49] licence with a 16-char holder) + index 49 [53] + board 2c + 8 first time
    + note 45+n [49+n] + hot row 77 (house colonies never have one in v6.1); the rest of the deposit (fee included) goes to the vault or credit pool; a row
    leaving the hot table frees 77 B to the contract.
  sell entry 97 B [105] from the proceeds (a NEAR sale must also cover 239 B for a possible refund); claim entry 96 B [104] from the NEAR (claims need > 335 B);
  tide entry 126 B + 4 B in the packed tide index (+45 on the tide that starts a 256-position page) = 130 B = 0.00130 NEAR from the vault per tide that runs [289 B];
  skip entry 62 B (contract); sponsor (first grant) 471 B for a guest (entry 78+La+Ls, grants record 77+La+Ls, Unsponsor reserve 210); slide 4x4 478 B, 16x16 509 B
    (record, owner list, entry, 184 reserve). The contract's own code: ~575036 B of wasm (v6.1 estimate; v6 570994) = 5.75 NEAR staked by the dish account.
Gas (NEAR protocol fee schedule; 1 TGas = 0.0001 NEAR at the minimum gas price): call 2.5 TGas + 60 Ggas per storage op + 12.2 Mgas per byte read / 70.7 Mgas
  per byte rewritten (value + evicted value + memory/registers) + 0.82 Mgas per wasm op. Hot table: loaded once per call that needs a colony's books (77 B a row
  read, ~600 ops a row to parse), the feed part (26 B) rewritten by every catch-up, the purse part (51 B) by tides and purse changes (~150 / 250 ops a row to write);
  a generation ~20k + 60 x live + 150 x colonies alive ops; a tide 4 writes + its 130 B + 60 ops a row + 400 per winner. A NEAR payout burns ~3.5 TGas and reserves
  30 TGas of the call's prepaid gas (counted in 'max gas'). v6.1 hot table: colonies that earned since the last tide, or that have living cells and a stake
  of at least STAKE_UNIT / reach = 125 (never the house), at most 10000 rows; at most 32 rows leave a call, each into its cold page (32 x 69 B). Catch-up limit
  min(110, prepaid - 40 - rows x 26 B x 72 Mgas - [rows x 51 B x 72 Mgas if a tide falls in the catch-up] - 32 x 0.35) TGas [v6: min(180, prepaid - 40 - rows x 77 x 64 M)].
  Prepaid on the site: 300 TGas for join, plant, buy, sell, claim, advance and mint_slide [v6: 200 / 250]; the house's agents unchanged (plant 200, buy/claim 150).
The house (agents/run.mjs, strategies.mjs, council.mjs), approximated:
  mainnet: 5 strains attach their own NEAR (0.55 NEAR each, kept above 0.12 for gas: they stop buying there), join with 0.05, buy 0.02 when they cannot afford
  12 cells, act every [40..100] x 8 generations (temperament ranges), at most 60% of their PETRI and 100 PETRI a day per planting budget (10 always allowed),
  lab notes, never a siege, never a licence; candidates per temperament (gardener: blocks/beehives/loaves, eater walls, pulsars; raider: gliders, glider guns, LWSS;
  engineer: eaters, gliders, guns; wanderer: outposts in the emptiest agar, acorns, pulsars; chaos: blinkers, toads, acorn/R-pentomino). The model's pick is
  approximated as an ambitious candidate with probability 1/3 when affordable, else the first ordinary one. Every 12 h a strain fossilizes its densest 16x16
  window (>= 12 cells). Council pacts move <= 3 PETRI house-to-house and change nothing outside the house: not simulated. The keeper (a house account) advances
  only once an outsider has joined and the dish is >= 400 generations behind; its tides and the strains' tides pay no bounty (it stays in the vault).
  testnet: strains pay with 1.5 NEAR of sponsored credit each at full pace; the 3-wallet bench plays by the written rules; the keeper (registered) advances
  at 20 behind or whenever the pot holds over 50 PETRI; relayer guests (0.05 NEAR credit each).
Players: winnings, sales and bounties go back into the wallet; claims once a day; after each tide sellers race for the allowance (bots first, then a random
  quarter of the rest selling above 16 cells' price). Every non-house player plants under a slide's licence when one exists for its pattern (25% off, a 10%
  royalty to the holder; v6.1: only slides of >= licence_min_cells cells, and a planter that never attached NEAR pays the royalty to the agar), in the slide's
  orientation. Kinds: see sim/src/world.rs `Kind` (Penny = min_join 0.01 NEAR, one block kept alive and paid). The Farmer mints only patterns that license.

=== 8. Per-scenario detail ===
  Columns per player (median over seeds of the kind's mean player): attached / sponsored / gas paid; claimed / sold / bounty / reclaimed received; cash and
  paper; credit won and left; royalties received and paid (PETRI); e/s = PETRI earned / spent; feed% and tide% = the kind's share of the feed and of tide shares.
